<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> SmoothScrollProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"div"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"ref"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * `true` eases the whole page (window). `false` (default) renders its own</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * scroll container — give it a height via className.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Smoothing per frame, 0–1. Lower is heavier. Ignored when `duration` is set. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  lerp</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Fixed ease duration in seconds for wheel and programmatic scrolls. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  duration</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  orientation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Orientation</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  wheelMultiplier</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Ease touch scrolling too. Off by default: native momentum feels right on phones. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  touch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Class for the inner content element (contained mode only). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  contentClassName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useEffect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(root </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { wrapper: wrapper</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, content: content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, eventsTarget: wrapper</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">      // A contained instance would otherwise swallow every wheel event over it,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">      // even at its ends, so the page stops scrolling under the pointer. At an</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">      // end, pushing outward, hand the event back to the browser so it chains</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">      // to the page like a native scroller does.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      virtualScroll: root</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> undefined</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">deltaX</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">deltaY</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
